[ReadControllerState]
; This uses the offical Nintendo Multi5 BIOS.
; Reserve some stack space for the BIOs to work and then check for an MPA in controller port 2.
; If there's no MPA then just load the auto-read registers for controllers 1 & 2. The MPA check will have already waited for the auto-read to complete.
; If an MPA was detected then call the CheckMPAState routine and load the state for controllers 1, 2 & 3 from the MPA read.
; This routine ignores a MPA plugged into controller port 1, input from controllers 4 & 5, and enabled flags for all controllers.
; This routine returns 0xFFFF in the Accumulator if an MPA was detected and 0x0000 if it was not.
0FFE00: 0B              PHD             ;Push Direct Page Register.
0FFE01: 08              PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
0FFE02: C230            REP #$30        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator & Index Registers.
0FFE04: 3B              TSC             ;Transfer Stack Pointer to Accumulator.
0FFE05: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
0FFE06: E91400          SBC #$0014      ;Subtract 0x0014 from the stack pointer to get a 20 byte section of stack space to process controller input.
0FFE09: 5B              TCD             ;Transfer Accumulator to Direct Page Register. So now DP points to $01DE on the stack.
0FFE0A: 1B              TCS             ;Transfer Accumulator to Stack Pointer.
0FFE0B: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
0FFE0D: 8B              PHB             ;Push Data Bank Register.
0FFE0E: A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
0FFE10: 48              PHA             ;Push 0x00 into the stack.
0FFE11: AB              PLB             ;Pull 0x00 into Data Bank Register.
0FFE12: 2070FE          JSR $FE70       ;Jump to CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection.
0FFE15: A501            LDA $01         ;Load {TempVar:MPAStatus} into Accumulator.
0FFE17: 8940            BIT #$40        ;Is a MultiPlayer 5 Adapter plugged into Port 2?
0FFE19: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
0FFE1B: F017            BEQ $FE34       ;Branch to ReadControllerState_NoMPA if no MultiPlayer 5 Adapter was detected in Port 2.
0FFE1D: 202AFF          JSR $FF2A       ;Jump to ReadMultiPlayer5AdapterState.
0FFE20: A50A            LDA $0A         ;Load {TempVar:JoyPad1State} into Accumulator.
0FFE22: 8D4200          STA $0042       ;Store {TempVar:JoyPad1State} into [JOYPAD1_BUTTON_STATE].
0FFE25: A508            LDA $08         ;Load {TempVar:JoyPad2State} into Accumulator.
0FFE27: 8D4400          STA $0044       ;Store {TempVar:JoyPad2State} into [JOYPAD2_BUTTON_STATE].
0FFE2A: A506            LDA $06         ;Load {TempVar:JoyPad3State} into Accumulator.
0FFE2C: 8D4600          STA $0046       ;Store {TempVar:JoyPad3State} into [JOYPAD3_BUTTON_STATE].
0FFE2F: A9FFFF          LDA #$FFFF      ;Load 0xFFFF into Accumulator. Return value. MPA was detected.
0FFE32: 8012            BRA $FE46       ;Branch to ReadControllerState_Exit.

[ReadControllerState_NoMPA]
0FFE34: AD1842          LDA $4218       ;Load [JOY1] into Accumulator.
0FFE37: 8D4200          STA $0042       ;Store [JOY1] into [JOYPAD1_BUTTON_STATE].
0FFE3A: AD1A42          LDA $421A       ;Load [JOY2] into Accumulator.
0FFE3D: 8D4400          STA $0044       ;Store [JOY2] into [JOYPAD2_BUTTON_STATE].
0FFE40: A90000          LDA #$0000      ;Load 0x0000 into Accumulator. With no MPA there is no JoyPad3.
0FFE43: 8D4600          STA $0046       ;Store 0x000 into [JOYPAD3_BUTTON_STATE].

[ReadControllerState_Exit]
0FFE46: AB              PLB             ;Pull Data Bank Register.
0FFE47: 8313            STA $13,S       ;Store the return value (00 == No NPA, FF == MPA) on the stack.
0FFE49: 3B              TSC             ;Transfer Stack Pointer to Accumulator.
0FFE4A: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
0FFE4B: 691200          ADC #$0012      ;Add 0x0012 to the stack pointer to restore the stack. The last two bytes will be popped into the Accumulator.
0FFE4E: 1B              TCS             ;Transfer Accumulator to Stack Pointer.
0FFE4F: 68              PLA             ;Pull the return value off the stack.
0FFE50: 28              PLP             ;Pull Processor Status Register.
0FFE51: 2B              PLD             ;Pull Direct Page Register.
0FFE52: 6B              RTL             ;Return Long.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[ASCIIString_Multi5ConnectStartString]
0FFE53: 5354415254204F46204D554C54493520434F4E4E45435420434845434B
         S T A R T   O F   M U L T I 5   C O N N E C T   C H E C K

[CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection]
; This routine will set {TempVar:MPAStatus} to one of 4 values:
; 0x00: No MPA connected in either port.
; 0x80: MPA connected to Port 1.
; 0x40: MPA connected to Port 2.
; 0xC0: MPA connected to Port 1 and Port 2.
0FFE70: 08              PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
0FFE71: E230            SEP #$30        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator & Index Registers.
0FFE73: 6401            STZ $01         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:MPAStatus}.

[CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_WaitForJoypadAutoRead]
0FFE75: AD1242          LDA $4212       ;Load [HVBJOY] into Accumulator.
0FFE78: 2901            AND #$01        ;Check for an in-progress Joypad auto-read.
0FFE7A: D0F9            BNE $FE75       ;Branch to CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_WaitForJoypadAutoRead if joypad state is still being updated.

[CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_Begin]
0FFE7C: 9C1640          STZ $4016       ;Store Zero into [JOYOUT].
0FFE7F: A901            LDA #$01        ;Load 0x01 into Accumulator.
0FFE81: 8D1640          STA $4016       ;Store 0x01 into [JOYOUT] to latch the button state.
0FFE84: A208            LDX #$08        ;Load 0x08 into Register X.

[CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_Signature0]
0FFE86: AD1640          LDA $4016       ;Load [JOYSER0] into Accumulator.
0FFE89: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift off the D0 bit.
0FFE8A: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift D1 into the carry flag.
0FFE8B: 2612            ROL $12         ;Roll D1 into {TempVar:WorkRegister0 (High)}.
0FFE8D: AD1740          LDA $4017       ;Load [JOYSER1] into Accumulator.
0FFE90: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift off the D0 bit.
0FFE91: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift D1 into the carry flag.
0FFE92: 2614            ROL $14         ;Roll D1 into {TempVar:WorkRegister1 (High)}.
0FFE94: CA              DEX             ;Decrement Register X.
0FFE95: D0EF            BNE $FE86       ;Branch to CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_Signature0 if all 8 bits haven't been read yet.
0FFE97: 9C1640          STZ $4016       ;Store Zero [JOYOUT].
0FFE9A: A208            LDX #$08        ;Load 0x08 into Register X.

[CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_Signature1]
0FFE9C: AD1640          LDA $4016       ;Load [JOYSER0] into Accumulator.
0FFE9F: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift off the D0 bit.
0FFEA0: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift D1 into the carry flag.
0FFEA1: 2611            ROL $11         ;Roll D1 into {TempVar:WorkRegister0 (Low)}.
0FFEA3: AD1740          LDA $4017       ;Load [JOYSER1] into Accumulator.
0FFEA6: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift off the D0 bit.
0FFEA7: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift D1 into the carry flag.
0FFEA8: 2613            ROL $13         ;Roll D1 into {TempVar:WorkRegister1 (Low)}.
0FFEAA: CA              DEX             ;Decrement Register X.
0FFEAB: D0EF            BNE $FE9C       ;Branch to CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_Signature1 if all 8 bits haven't been read yet.

[CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_TestPort1ForMPA]
0FFEAD: A512            LDA $12         ;Load {TempVar:WorkRegister0 (High)} into Accumulator.
0FFEAF: C9FF            CMP #$FF        ;Is a special device connected?
0FFEB1: D00A            BNE $FEBD       ;If no device is connected then branch to CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_TestPort2ForMPA.
0FFEB3: A511            LDA $11         ;Load {TempVar:WorkRegister0 (Low)} into Accumulator.
0FFEB5: C9FF            CMP #$FF        ;Is the special device a MultiPlayer 5 Adapter?
0FFEB7: F004            BEQ $FEBD       ;If the device isn't the MultiPlayer 5 Adapter then branch to CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_TestPort2ForMPA.
0FFEB9: A980            LDA #$80        ;Load 0x80 into Accumulator to indicate an MPA is connected to Port 1.
0FFEBB: 8501            STA $01         ;Store 0x80 into {TempVar:MPAStatus}.

[CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_TestPort2ForMPA]
0FFEBD: A514            LDA $14         ;Load {TempVar:WorkRegister1 (High)} into Accumulator.
0FFEBF: C9FF            CMP #$FF        ;Is a special device connected?
0FFEC1: D00C            BNE $FECF       ;If no device is connected then branch to CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_Exit.
0FFEC3: A513            LDA $13         ;Load {TempVar:WorkRegister1 (Low)} into Accumulator.
0FFEC5: C9FF            CMP #$FF        ;Is the special device a MultiPlayer 5 Adapter?
0FFEC7: F006            BEQ $FECF       ;If the device isn't the MultiPlayer 5 Adapter then branch to CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_Exit.
0FFEC9: A940            LDA #$40        ;Load 0x40 into Accumulator to indicate an MPA is connected to Port 2.
0FFECB: 0501            ORA $01         ;Logical OR with {TempVar:MPAStatus} to set the Port 2 device flag.
0FFECD: 8501            STA $01         ;Store the status back into {TempVar:MPAStatus}.

[CheckMultiPlayer5AdapterConnection_Exit]
0FFECF: 28              PLP             ;Pull Processor Status Register.
0FFED0: 60              RTS             ;Return.

         S T A R T   O F   M U L T I 5   B I O S

[ReadMultiPlayer5AdapterState]
0FFF2A: 08              PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
0FFF2B: E230            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
0FFF2D: 6401            STZ $01         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:MPAStatus}.

[ReadMultiPlayer5AdapterState_WaitForJoypadAutoRead]
0FFF2F: AD1242          LDA $4212       ;Load [HVBJOY] into Accumulator.
0FFF32: 2901            AND #$01        ;Check for an in-progress Joypad auto-read.
0FFF34: D0F9            BNE $FF2F       ;Branch to ReadMultiPlayer5AdapterState_WaitForJoypadAutoRead if joypad state is still being updated.

; Read the state of controllers 1, 2 and 3.
0FFF36: AD1942          LDA $4219       ;Load [JOY1H] into Accumulator.
0FFF39: 850B            STA $0B         ;Store [JOY1H] into {TempVar:JoyPad1State (High)}.
0FFF3B: AD1842          LDA $4218       ;Load [JOY1L] into Accumulator.
0FFF3E: 850A            STA $0A         ;Store [JOY1L] into {TempVar:JoyPad1State (Low)}.
0FFF40: 290F            AND #$0F        ;Drop any bits not part of the controller signature.
0FFF42: 8510            STA $10         ;Store result into {TempVar:JoyPad1Connected}.
0FFF44: AD1640          LDA $4016       ;Load [JOYSER0] into Accumulator.
0FFF47: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift the enabled bit into the carry flag.
0FFF48: 2610            ROL $10         ;Store the enabled bit into {TempVar:JoyPad1Connected}.
0FFF4A: AD1B42          LDA $421B       ;Load [JOY2H] into Accumulator.
0FFF4D: 8509            STA $09         ;Store [JOY2H] into {TempVar:JoyPad2State (High)}.
0FFF4F: AD1A42          LDA $421A       ;Load [JOY2L] into Accumulator.
0FFF52: 8508            STA $08         ;Store [JOY2L] into {TempVar:JoyPad2State (Low)}.
0FFF54: 290F            AND #$0F        ;Drop any bits not part of the controller signature.
0FFF56: 850F            STA $0F         ;Store result into {TempVar:JoyPad2Connected}.
0FFF58: AD1F42          LDA $421F       ;Load [JOY4H] into Accumulator.
0FFF5B: 8507            STA $07         ;Store [JOY4H] into {TempVar:JoyPad3State (High)}.
0FFF5D: AD1E42          LDA $421E       ;Load [JOY4L] into Accumulator.
0FFF60: 8506            STA $06         ;Store [JOY4L] into {TempVar:JoyPad3State (Low)}.
0FFF62: 290F            AND #$0F        ;Drop any bits not part of the controller signature.
0FFF64: 850E            STA $0E         ;Store result into {TempVar:JoyPad3Connected}.
0FFF66: AD1740          LDA $4017       ;Load [JOYSER1] into Accumulator.
0FFF69: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift the enabled bit into the carry flag.
0FFF6A: 260F            ROL $0F         ;Store the enabled bit into {TempVar:JoyPad2Connected}.
0FFF6C: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift the enabled bit into the carry flag.
0FFF6D: 260E            ROL $0E         ;Store the enabled bit into {TempVar:JoyPad3Connected}.
0FFF6F: 9C0142          STZ $4201       ;Store Zero into [WRIO] to switch to controllers 4 and 5.
0FFF72: A010            LDY #$10        ;Load 0x10 into Register Y.

[ReadMultiPlayer5AdapterState_ReadJoypad4And5State]
0FFF74: AD1740          LDA $4017       ;Load [JOYSER1] into Accumulator.
0FFF77: C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
0FFF79: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift the joypad 4 button bit into the carry flag.
0FFF7A: 2604            ROL $04         ;Roll the controller button bit into {TempVar:JoyPad4State}.
0FFF7C: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift the joypad 5 button bit into the carry flag.
0FFF7D: 2602            ROL $02         ;Roll the controller button bit into {TempVar:JoyPad5State}.
0FFF7F: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
0FFF81: 88              DEY             ;Decrement Register Y.
0FFF82: D0F0            BNE $FF74       ;Branch to ReadMultiPlayer5AdapterState_ReadJoypad4And5State if Y > 0.
0FFF84: A504            LDA $04         ;Load {TempVar:JoyPad4State} into Accumulator.
0FFF86: 290F            AND #$0F        ;Drop everything but the signature bits.
0FFF88: 850D            STA $0D         ;Store the signature in {TempVar:JoyPad5Connected}.
0FFF8A: A502            LDA $02         ;Load {TempVar:JoyPad5State} into Accumulator.
0FFF8C: 290F            AND #$0F        ;Drop everything but the signature bits.
0FFF8E: 850C            STA $0C         ;Store the signature in {TempVar:JoyPad5Connected}.
0FFF90: AD1740          LDA $4017       ;Load [JOYSER1] into Accumulator.
0FFF93: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift Joypad 4 controller enabled bit into the carry flag.
0FFF94: 260D            ROL $0D         ;Store the enabled bit into {TempVar:JoyPad4Connected}.
0FFF96: 4A              LSR A           ;Shift Joypad 5 controller enabled bit into the carry flag.
0FFF97: 260C            ROL $0C         ;Store the enabled bit into {TempVar:JoyPad5Connected}.
0FFF99: A980            LDA #$80        ;Load 0x80 into Accumulator.
0FFF9B: 8D0142          STA $4201       ;Store 0x80 into [WRIO] to switch to controllers 2 and 3.
0FFF9E: 28              PLP             ;Pull Processor Status Register.
0FFF9F: 60              RTS             ;Return.
